2
, (
49
) and consistency of
\displaystyle\hat{\beta}_{S,n}
that if
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Bigl[\mathcal{Q}_{S_{2}S_{2},2}\bigl(\beta^{\dagger}_{S}+\lambda(\hat{\beta}_{S,n}-\beta^{\dagger}_{S}),\beta_{R},\sigma\bigr)\Bigr]_{j_{1}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\int\tfrac{1}{2}\bigl(\partial_{j_{1}}^{\beta_{S_{2}}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}S_{1}}\bigl(x,(\beta^{\dagger}_{S},\beta_{R},\sigma)\bigr)\partial_{j_{2}}^{\beta_{S_{2}}}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,(\beta^{\dagger}_{S},\beta_{R},\sigma))\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\int\bigl(\partial_{j_{1}}^{\beta_{S_{2}}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}S_{2}}\bigl(x,(\beta^{\dagger}_{S},\beta_{R},\sigma)\bigr)\partial_{j_{2}}^{\beta_{S_{2}}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}})\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx);

(52)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Bigl[\mathcal{Q}_{S_{2}S_{2},k}\bigl(\beta^{\dagger}_{S}+\lambda(\hat{\beta}_{S,n}-\beta^{\dagger}_{S}),\beta_{R},\sigma\bigr)\Bigr]_{j_{1}j_{2}}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,\qquad k=1,3,4.

The proof is complete by applying Lemma
5
to (
51
) and (
52
).

D.5
Proof of Lemma
11

It holds that
\displaystyle\textstyle{\tfrac{1}{n}\ell_{n}(\theta)=\sum_{1\leq k\leq 4}\mathscr{T}_{k}(\theta)}
,
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
, where we have set:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{T}_{1}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N_{S_{1}}}R_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{1}S_{1}}(1,X_{{i-1}},\theta)\eta_{S_{1},i-1}^{j_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}},\beta_{S_{1}})\eta_{S_{1},i-1}^{j_{2}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}},\beta_{S_{1}})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq N_{S_{1}}\\
1\leq j_{2}\leq N_{S_{2}}\end{subarray}}R_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{1}S_{2}}(1,X_{{i-1}},\theta)\eta_{S_{1},i-1}^{j_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}},\beta_{S_{1}})\eta_{S_{2},i-1}^{j_{2}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},\beta_{S_{2}})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N_{S_{2}}}R_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{2}S_{2}}(1,X_{{i-1}},\theta)\eta_{S_{2},i-1}^{j_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},\beta_{S_{2}})\eta_{S_{2},i-1}^{j_{2}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},\beta_{S_{2}})\biggr\};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{T}_{2}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq N_{S_{1}}\\
1\leq j_{2}\leq N\end{subarray}}{R}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{1}}(1,X_{{i-1}},\theta)\eta_{S_{1},i-1}^{j_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}},\beta_{S_{1}})m_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq N_{S_{2}}\\
1\leq j_{2}\leq N\end{subarray}}{R}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{2}}(1,X_{{i-1}},\theta)\eta_{S_{2},i-1}^{j_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},\beta_{S_{2}})m_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{1\leq j\leq N_{S_{1}}}{R}_{j}^{S_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta)\eta_{S_{1},i-1}^{j}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}},\beta_{S_{1}})+\sum_{1\leq j\leq N_{S_{2}}}{R}_{j}^{S_{2}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta)\eta_{S_{2},i-1}^{j}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},\beta_{S_{2}})\biggr\};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{T}_{3}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\Lambda\bigl(X_{{i-1}},(\beta_{S},\sigma)\bigr)m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})+\log\bigl|\Sigma\bigl(X_{{i-1}},(\beta_{S},\sigma)\bigr)\bigr|\biggr\};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{T}_{4}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{\sum_{1\leq j\leq N}R_{j}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta)m_{i}^{j}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})+R(\Delta_{n},X_{{i-1}},\theta)\biggr\},

where
\displaystyle R_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{1}S_{1}},\,R_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{1}S_{2}},\,R_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{2}S_{2}},\,R_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{1}},\,R_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{S_{2}},\,R_{j}^{S_{1}},\,R_{j}^{S_{2}},\,R_{j_{1}j_{2}}\,R_{j},R\in\mathcal{S}
.
From Lemmas
2
,
3
and the convergences (
43
) and (
44
), we have:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{T}_{k}\bigl(\hat{\beta}_{S,n},\beta_{R},\sigma\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,\qquad k=1,2,4;

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{T}_{3}\bigl(\hat{\beta}_{S,n},\beta_{R},\sigma\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\int\Bigl\{\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\Lambda\bigl(x,(\beta^{\dagger}_{S},\sigma)\bigr)\Sigma\bigl(x,(\beta^{\dagger}_{S},\sigma^{\dagger})\bigr)\bigr)+\log\bigl|\Sigma\bigl(x,(\beta^{\dagger}_{S},\sigma)\bigr)\bigr|\Bigr\}\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, uniformly in
\displaystyle(\beta_{R},\sigma)\in\Theta_{\beta_{R}}\times\Theta_{\sigma}
. The proof is now complete.

D.6
Proof of Lemma
12

The matrix-valued function
\displaystyle\Sigma(x,\theta)
and its inverse
\displaystyle\Lambda(x,\theta)
depend on
\displaystyle\beta_{S}=(\beta_{S_{1}},\beta_{S_{2}})\in\Theta_{\beta_{S}}
and
\displaystyle\sigma\in\Theta_{\sigma}
but not on
\displaystyle\beta_{R}\in\Theta_{\beta_{R}}
in terms of the parameter
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
. We then define

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{L}(\theta)=\tfrac{1}{n\Delta_{n}}\ell_{n}(\theta)-\tfrac{1}{n\Delta_{n}}\ell_{n}\bigl(\beta_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{R},\sigma\bigr),\quad\theta=(\beta_{S},\beta_{R},\sigma)\in\Theta,

and
\displaystyle\mathscr{L}(\theta)
is expressed as

\displaystyle\textstyle{\mathscr{L}(\theta)=\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}\mathscr{U}_{k}(\theta)}
, where we have set:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{1}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{\Bigl(m_{i}(\Delta_{n},(\beta_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{R},\sigma))-m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\Bigr)^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},(\beta_{S},\sigma))\Bigl(m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{i}(\Delta_{n},(\beta_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{R},\sigma))\Bigr)\biggr\};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{2}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{\Bigl(m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\Bigr)^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},(\beta_{S},\sigma))\Bigl(m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{i}(\Delta_{n},(\beta_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{R},\sigma))\Bigr)\biggr\};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{3}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{2}{n\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},(\beta_{S},\sigma))\bigl(m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{i}(\Delta_{n},(\beta_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{R},\sigma))\bigr).

We will derive the limit of the terms
\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{k}(\theta)
,
\displaystyle 1\leq k\leq 3
evaluated at

\displaystyle\theta=(\hat{\beta}_{S,n},\,\beta_{R},\,\hat{\sigma}_{n})
by utilising
Lemma
6
:
We first consider the term
\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{1}(\theta)
. Making use of Lemma
6
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathscr{U}_{1}(\theta)&=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{\sum_{1\leq j\leq N_{S_{1}}}\eta_{S_{1},i-1}^{j}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}},\beta_{S_{1}})R^{j}_{S_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta)\\[5.69046pt]
&\qquad+\sum_{1\leq j\leq N_{S_{2}}}\eta_{S_{2},i-1}^{j}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},\beta_{S_{2}})R^{j}_{S_{2}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta)+R(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta)\biggr\},\end{aligned}

(53)

where
\displaystyle R_{S_{1}}^{j},\,R_{S_{2}}^{j},\,R\in\mathcal{S}
. From Lemma
2
and the limits (
43
), (
44
), we obtain that if
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{1}\big(\hat{\beta}_{S,n},\,\beta_{R},\,\hat{\sigma}_{n}\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

uniformly in
\displaystyle\beta_{R}\in\Theta_{\beta_{R}}
. For the term
\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{2}(\theta)
, again Lemma
6
yields

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{2}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\eta_{R}(X_{{i-1}},\beta_{R})^{\top}a_{R}^{-1}(X_{{i-1}},\sigma)\eta_{R}(X_{{i-1}},\beta_{R})+\widetilde{\mathscr{U}}_{2}(\theta),

where
\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{U}}_{2}(\theta)
is given in the form of the right-hand side of formula (
53
). We then obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{2}\big(\hat{\beta}_{S,n},\,\beta_{R},\,\hat{\sigma}_{n}\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\int\eta_{R}(x,\beta_{R})^{\top}\,a_{R}^{-1}(x,\sigma^{\dagger})\,\eta_{R}(x,\beta_{R})\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, uniformly in
\displaystyle\beta_{R}\in\Theta_{\beta_{R}}
.
For the third term
\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{3}(\theta)
, it follows from Lemma
6
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{3}(\theta)=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{N_{S}+1\leq j\leq N}m_{i}^{j}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})R^{j}(1,X_{{i-1}},\theta)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq j\leq N}m_{i}^{j}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\widetilde{R}^{j}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta),

where
\displaystyle R^{j},\,\widetilde{R}^{j}\in\mathcal{S}
. From Lemma
3
, we have that, if
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{U}_{3}(\hat{\beta}_{S,n},\beta_{R},\hat{\sigma}_{n})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

uniformly in
\displaystyle\beta_{R}\in\Theta_{\beta_{R}}
. The proof is now complete.

D.7
Proof of Lemma
13

Recall
\displaystyle N_{\theta}=N_{\beta}+N_{\sigma}
, where
\displaystyle N_{\beta}=N_{\beta_{S}}+N_{\beta_{R}}
with
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S}}=N_{\beta_{S_{1}}}+N_{\beta_{S_{2}}}
. In this section we make use of the notation
\displaystyle\beta=(\beta_{S},\beta_{R})\in\Theta_{\beta_{S}}\times\Theta_{\beta_{R}}
with
\displaystyle\beta_{S}=(\beta_{S_{1}},\beta_{S_{2}})
. We note again that the matrices
\displaystyle\Sigma(x,\theta)
and
\displaystyle\Lambda(x,\theta)
do not depend on the parameter
\displaystyle\beta_{R}
. Since we have seen the convergences of the matrices
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{S_{1}S_{1}}(\theta),\,\mathcal{Q}_{S_{1}S_{2}}(\theta),\,\mathcal{Q}_{S_{2}S_{2}}(\theta)
evaluated at
\displaystyle\theta=\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger}),\,\lambda\in[0,1]
in Appendix
D.4
, we prove that as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, the following convergences hold uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in[0,1]
:

(a)

For
\displaystyle 1\leq i\leq N_{\beta}
,
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq j\leq N_{\beta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}

(54)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\begin{cases}2\int\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{R,0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{R})\bigr)^{\top}\,a_{R}^{-1}(x,\sigma^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{R})\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),&N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq i,j\leq N_{\theta};\\[8.5359pt]
0,&(\mathrm{otherwise}).\end{cases}

(b)

For
\displaystyle 1\leq i\leq N_{\beta}
,
\displaystyle N_{\beta}+1\leq j\leq N_{\theta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0.

(55)

(c)

For
\displaystyle N_{\beta}+1\leq i,j\leq N_{\theta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\int\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\sigma}_{i}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\partial^{\sigma}_{j}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

(56)

D.7.1
Proof of (
54
)

We consider the following three cases separately:

(a1)

\displaystyle 1\leq i\leq N_{\beta_{S_{1}}},\;N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq j\leq N_{\beta}
;

(a2)

\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S_{1}}}+1\leq i\leq N_{\beta_{S}},\;N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq j\leq N_{\beta}
;

(a3)

\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq i,j\leq N_{\beta}
.

In case (a1), we have for
\displaystyle\theta=(\beta_{S_{1}},\beta_{S_{2}},\beta_{R},\sigma)\in\Theta
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}H_{ij}(X_{{m-1}},\theta)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}R(\Delta_{n},X_{{m-1}},\theta)

(57)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq k\leq N_{S_{1}}}R_{k}(1,X_{{m-1}},\theta)\bigl(V_{S_{1},0}^{k}(X_{{S,m-1}},\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{1}})-V_{S_{1},0}^{k}(X_{{S,m-1}},\beta_{S_{1}})\bigr),

for some
\displaystyle R_{k},R\in\mathcal{S},\,1\leq k\leq N_{S_{1}}
, where we have defined
\displaystyle H_{ij}(x,\theta),\,(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta
as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle H_{ij}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{3}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S_{1}},\beta_{S_{1}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)+\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S_{1}},\beta_{S_{1}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{2}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S_{1}},\beta_{S_{1}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}R}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R}).

Noticing that for
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq j\leq N_{\beta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)=\partial_{x_{R}}^{\top}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta_{S_{2}})\partial_{j}^{\beta}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R}),\quad\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)=\partial_{x_{S_{2}}}^{\top}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S},\beta_{S_{1}})\partial_{x_{R}}^{\top}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta_{S_{2}})\partial_{j}^{\beta}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R}),

we have
\displaystyle H_{ij}(x,\theta)=0
for any
\displaystyle(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta
since

\displaystyle\displaystyle H_{ij}(x,\theta)=2\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\beta_{S_{1}})\bigr)^{\top}\widetilde{H}_{ij}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R}),

where

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{ij}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\bigl\{\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1}}(x,\theta)\Sigma_{S_{1}R}(x,\theta)+\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{2}}(x,\theta)\Sigma_{S_{2}R}(x,\theta)+\Lambda_{S_{1}R}(x,\theta)\Sigma_{RR}(x,\sigma)\bigr\}a_{R}^{-1}(x,\sigma)=\mathbf{0}_{N_{S_{1}}\times N_{R}}.

Thus, due to the consistency of the estimator and Lemma
2
, we immediately obtain from (
57
) that if
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)]_{ij}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in[0,1]
for
\displaystyle 1\leq i\leq N_{\beta_{S_{1}}},\;N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq j\leq N_{\beta}
.

Subsequently, we consider the case (a2). We have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\widetilde{H}_{ij,1}(X_{{m-1}},\theta)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\widetilde{H}_{ij,2}(X_{{m-1}},\theta)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq k\leq N_{S_{1}}}R_{k}(1,X_{{m-1}},\theta)\eta_{S_{1},m-1}^{k}(\Delta_{n},\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{N_{S_{1}}+1\leq k\leq N_{S}}{R}_{k}(1,X_{{m-1}},\theta)\bigl(V_{S,0}^{k}(X_{{m-1}},\beta^{\dagger}_{S})-V_{S,0}^{k}(X_{{m-1}},\beta_{S})\bigr)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}R(\Delta_{n},X_{{m-1}},\theta),

for some
\displaystyle R_{k},R\in\mathcal{S},\,1\leq k\leq N_{S}
, where we have defined
\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{ij,k}(x,\theta)
, for
\displaystyle(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta
,
\displaystyle k=1,2
, as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{ij,1}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\equiv\tfrac{1}{6}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)+\tfrac{1}{2}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{2}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}R}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R});

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{ij,2}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\equiv\bigl(\tfrac{1}{3}\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}S_{1}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)+\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}S_{2}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}R}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R}).

We then have
\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{ij,k}(x,\theta)=0
for any
\displaystyle(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta
,
\displaystyle k=1,2
, from the same argument as in case (a1). Thus, making use of Lemma
2
, convergence (
43
) with condition (
C2
) and the consistency of the estimator, we obtain (
54
) in case (a2).

Finally, we consider the case (a3). We have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\bar{H}_{ij,1}(X_{{k-1}},\theta)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\bar{H}_{ij,2}(X_{{k-1}},\theta)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\bar{H}_{ij,3}(X_{{k-1}},\theta)+\tfrac{1}{n\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}R(1,X_{{k-1}},\theta)\,m_{k}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{n\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\bigl(m_{k}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{k}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{k-1}},\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{(i,j)}v(X_{{k-1}},\theta)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\widetilde{R}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{k-1}},\theta),

for
\displaystyle R,\,\widetilde{R}\in\mathcal{S}
, where we have set, for
\displaystyle(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \bar{H}_{ij,1}(x,\theta)&\equiv\tfrac{1}{18}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\\[5.69046pt]
&\quad+\tfrac{1}{6}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{2}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)\\[5.69046pt]
&\quad+\tfrac{1}{3}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}R}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R});\\[8.5359pt]
\end{aligned}

(58)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \bar{H}_{ij,2}(x,\theta)&\equiv\tfrac{1}{6}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}S_{1}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\\[5.69046pt]
&\quad+\tfrac{1}{2}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}S_{2}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)\\[5.69046pt]
&\quad+\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}R}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R});\\[8.5359pt]
\end{aligned}

(59)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \bar{H}_{ij,3}(x,\theta)&\equiv\tfrac{1}{3}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{RS_{1}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\\[5.69046pt]
&\quad+\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{RS_{2}}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)\\[5.69046pt]
&\quad+2\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{RR}(x,\theta)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R}),\end{aligned}

(60)

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle v(x,\theta)\equiv\Bigl[\tfrac{1}{6}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)^{\top},\,\tfrac{1}{2}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)^{\top},\,V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R})^{\top}\Bigr]^{\top}.

Notice that for any
\displaystyle(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bar{H}_{ij,1}(x,\theta)=0,\qquad\bar{H}_{ij,2}(x,\theta)=0,\qquad\bar{H}_{ij,3}(x,\theta)=2\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R})\bigr)^{\top}a_{R}^{-1}(x,\sigma)\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R}).

Furthermore, it follows that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\bigl(m_{k}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{k}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{k-1}},\theta)\partial^{\beta}_{(i,j)}v(X_{{k-1}},\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\bigl(V_{R,0}(X_{{k-1}},\beta^{\dagger}_{R})-V_{R,0}(X_{{k-1}},\beta_{R})\bigr)^{\top}a_{R}^{-1}(X_{{k-1}},\sigma)\partial^{\beta}_{(i,j)}V_{R,0}(X_{{k-1}},\beta_{R}),

where we made use of similar arguments in the proof of Lemma
6
in Appendix
A.2
for the term
\displaystyle\Lambda(X_{{k-1}},\theta)\partial^{\beta}_{(i,j)}v(X_{{k-1}},\theta)
. Hence, exploiting Lemmas
2
,
3
and the consistency of estimator
\displaystyle\hat{\theta}_{n}
, we obtain that:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\Bigr]_{ij}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\int\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{i}V_{R,0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{R})\bigr)^{\top}\,a_{R}^{-1}(x,\sigma^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{j}V_{R,0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{R})\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, for
\displaystyle N_{\beta}+1\leq i,j\leq N_{\theta}
.
The proof of (
54
) is now complete.

D.7.2
Proof of (
55
)

We show (
55
) when
\displaystyle 1\leq i\leq N_{\beta_{S_{1}}}
and
\displaystyle N_{\beta}+1\leq j\leq N_{\theta}
. The convergence for the other cases can be deduced from a similar argument used in the proof of (
54
) so we omit the proof. We have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}}}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N}R_{k_{1}k_{2}}(1,X_{{k-1}},\theta)m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})m_{k}^{k_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}}}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N}\biggl\{\widetilde{R}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(1,X_{{k-1}},\theta)\bigl(m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\bigl(m_{k}^{k_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{k}^{k_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\biggr\}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq k_{1}\leq N}{R}_{k_{1}}(1,X_{{k-1}},\theta)\bigl(m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta)-m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{R}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{k-1}},\theta),

for some
\displaystyle R_{k_{1}k_{2}},\widetilde{R}_{k_{1}k_{2}},R_{k_{1}},R\in\mathcal{S}
.
Thus, we immediately obtain (
55
) for
\displaystyle 1\leq i\leq N_{\beta_{S_{1}}},\,N_{\beta}+1\leq j\leq N_{\theta}
from Lemma
2
,
3
and (
43
)-(
44
).

D.7.3
Proof of (
56
)

It holds that for
\displaystyle N_{\beta}+1\leq i,j\leq N_{\theta}
,
\displaystyle\theta=(\beta_{S_{1}},\beta_{S_{2}},\beta_{R},\sigma)\in\Theta
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}m_{k}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\,\partial^{\sigma}_{(i,j)}\Lambda(X_{{k-1}},\theta)\,m_{k}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\partial^{\sigma}_{(i,j)}\log\bigl|\Sigma(X_{{k-1}},\theta)\bigr|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N}R_{k_{1}k_{2}}(1,X_{{k-1}},\theta)m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\bigl(m_{k}^{k_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})-m_{k}^{k_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta)\bigr)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N}\biggl\{\widetilde{R}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(1,X_{{k-1}},\theta)\bigl(m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})-m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta)\bigr)\bigl(m_{k}^{k_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})-m_{k}^{k_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta)\bigr)\biggr\}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{1\leq k_{1}\leq N}R_{k_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{k-1}},\theta)\bigl(m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})-m_{k}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta)\bigr)+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}R(\Delta_{n},X_{{k-1}},\theta),

for some
\displaystyle R_{k_{1}k_{2}},\widetilde{R}_{k_{1}k_{2}},R_{k_{1}},R\in\mathcal{S}
. Making use of Lemmas
2
–
3
, (
43
), (
44
) and the consistency of the estimator, we obtain as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bigl[\mathscr{I}_{n}\bigl(\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\bigr]_{ij}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\int\Bigl\{\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\sigma}_{(i,j)}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)+\partial^{\sigma}_{(i,j)}\log\bigl|\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr|\Bigr\}\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad=\int\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\sigma}_{i}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\partial^{\sigma}_{j}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in[0,1]
, where we applied the following two formulae to the above equation:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial^{\sigma}_{(i,j)}\log\bigl|\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr|=-\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\sigma}_{(i,j)}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)-\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\sigma}_{i}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\partial^{\sigma}_{j}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\sigma}_{i}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\partial^{\sigma}_{j}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)=-\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\sigma}_{i}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\partial^{\sigma}_{j}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr).

The proof is now complete.

D.8
Proof of Lemma
14

We write

\displaystyle\mathscr{C}_{n}^{k}(\theta)=\textstyle{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\zeta_{i}^{k}(\theta),\;\theta\in\Theta,\;1\leq k\leq N_{\theta}}
,
where we have set:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\zeta_{i}^{k}(\theta)\equiv\bigl[M_{n}^{-1}\bigr]_{kk}\times\partial^{\theta}_{k}\bigl\{m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},\theta)m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)+\log|\Sigma(X_{{i-1}},\theta)|\bigr\}.

To prove the assertion, it suffices to show, from Theorem 3.2 and 3.4 in
[
17
]
, that:

(i)

If
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
with
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}=o(n^{-1/2})
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[\zeta_{i}^{k}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,\quad 1\leq k\leq N_{\theta}.

(61)

(ii)

If
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[\zeta_{i}^{k_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})\zeta_{i}^{k_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}4[\Gamma(\theta^{\dagger})]_{k_{1}k_{2}},\quad 1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N_{\theta}.

(62)

(iii)

If
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[\zeta_{i}^{k_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})\zeta_{i}^{k_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})\zeta_{i}^{k_{3}}(\theta^{\dagger})\zeta_{i}^{k_{4}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,\quad 1\leq k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}\leq N_{\theta}.

(63)

In what follows, we will check convergences (
61
) and (
62
). One can prove (
63
) following similar arguments and by noticing that the left-hand-side of (
63
) involves
\displaystyle 1/n^{2}
.

D.8.1
Proof of (
61
)

We recall (
32
) and (
33
) that are immediately obtained from the definition of
\displaystyle m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})
in (
28
), that is, for
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[m_{i}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]=R_{1}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger});

(64)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[m_{i}^{k_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})m_{i}^{k_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]=[\Sigma(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})]_{k_{1}k_{2}}+R_{2}({\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})

(65)

for
\displaystyle R_{1},R_{2}\in\mathcal{S}
. We then write
\displaystyle\zeta_{i}^{k}(\theta),\,\theta\in\Theta,\,1\leq i\leq n,\,1\leq k\leq N_{\theta}
as
\displaystyle\zeta_{i}^{k}(\theta)=\zeta_{i,1}^{k}(\theta)+\zeta_{i,2}^{k}(\theta)
, where we have set:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\zeta_{i,1}^{k}(\theta)=2\bigl[M_{n}^{-1}\bigr]_{kk}\times\bigl\{\bigl(\partial^{\theta}_{k}m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},\theta)\,m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)\bigr\};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\zeta_{i,2}^{k}(\theta)=\bigl[M_{n}^{-1}\bigr]_{kk}\times\bigl\{\partial^{\theta}_{k}\log|\Sigma(X_{{i-1}},\theta)|+m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)^{\top}\partial^{\theta}_{k}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},\theta)\,m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta)\bigr\}.

Exploiting (
64
), we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[\zeta_{i,1}^{k}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}R_{k}^{j}(1,X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\,\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[m_{i}^{j}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]=\tfrac{1}{n}\widetilde{R}_{k}^{j}(\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{3}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger}),

for
\displaystyle R_{k}^{j},\,\widetilde{R}_{k}^{j}\in\mathcal{S}
.
Thus, from Lemma
2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[\zeta_{i,1}^{k}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{R}_{k}^{j}(\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{3}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

if
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
and
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}=o(n^{-1/2})
. Next, we consider the second term
\displaystyle\zeta_{i,2}^{k}(\theta)
. First, notice that for
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq k\leq N_{\beta}
,
\displaystyle\zeta_{i,2}^{k}(\theta^{\dagger})=0
, since
\displaystyle\Sigma(X_{{i-1}},\theta)
and
\displaystyle\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},\theta)
are independent of
\displaystyle\beta_{R}\in\Theta_{\beta_{R}}
. For
\displaystyle 1\leq k\leq N_{\beta_{S}}
and
\displaystyle N_{\beta}+1\leq k\leq N_{\theta}
, we apply (
65
) to obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[\zeta^{k}_{i,2}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=[M_{n}^{-1}]_{kk}\times\Bigl\{\partial^{\theta}_{k}\log|\Sigma(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})|+\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\theta}_{k}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\Sigma(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\Bigr\}+\tfrac{1}{n}R_{k}(\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{2}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}R_{k}(\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{2}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})

for
\displaystyle R_{k}\in\mathcal{S}
, where we used:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial^{\theta}_{k}\log|\Sigma(x,\theta)|=-\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\theta}_{k}\Lambda(x,\theta)\Sigma(x,\theta)\bigr),\quad(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta.

(66)

Thus, we have from Lemma
2
that if
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
with
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}=o(n^{-1/2})
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[\zeta_{i,2}^{k}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{k}(\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{2}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

and the proof of (
61
) is now complete.

D.8.2
Proof of (
62
)

For simplicity, we write

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{Y}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})\equiv\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[\zeta_{i}^{k_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})\zeta_{i}^{k_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr].

We have that for
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1}\leq N_{\beta_{S}}
,
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq k_{2}\leq N_{\beta}
,
\displaystyle N_{\beta}+1\leq k_{3}\leq N_{\theta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sqrt{n}\zeta_{i}^{k_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})&=-2\,\mu_{k_{1}}(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\;m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})\\[5.69046pt]
&\quad+\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}R_{k_{1}}^{j_{1}j_{2}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})m_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})m_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq N}R_{k_{1}}^{j_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})m_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger});\end{aligned}

(67)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sqrt{n}\zeta_{i}^{k_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})&=-2\,\mu_{k_{2}}(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger});\end{aligned}

(68)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sqrt{n}\zeta_{i}^{k_{3}}(\theta^{\dagger})&=m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\,\bigl(\partial^{\theta}_{k_{3}}\Lambda(X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\,m_{i}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})+\partial^{\theta}_{k_{3}}\log|\Sigma(X_{{i-1}}\theta^{\dagger})|\\[5.69046pt]
&\quad+\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}R_{k_{3}}^{j_{1}j_{2}}({\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\,m_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})m_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger})+\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}R_{k_{3}}^{j_{1}}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},X_{{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\,m_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta_{n},\theta^{\dagger}),\end{aligned}

(69)

for
\displaystyle R_{k_{1}}^{j_{1}j_{2}},\,R_{k_{1}}^{j_{1}},\,R_{k_{3}}^{j_{1}j_{2}},\,R_{k_{3}}^{j_{1}}\in\mathcal{S}
, where we defined
\displaystyle\mu_{k}:\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta\to\mathbb{R}^{N},\,1\leq k\leq N_{\beta}
as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mu_{k}(x,\theta)=\begin{cases}\Bigl[\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S},\beta_{S_{1}})\bigr)^{\top},\,\mathbf{0}_{N_{S_{2}}}^{\top},\,\mathbf{0}_{N_{R}}^{\top}\Bigr]^{\top},&1\leq k\leq N_{\beta_{S_{1}}};\\[5.69046pt]
\Bigl[\tfrac{1}{2}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top},\,\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top},\,\mathbf{0}_{N_{R}}^{\top}\Bigr]^{\top},&N_{\beta_{S_{1}}}+1\leq k\leq N_{\beta_{S}};\\[5.69046pt]
\Bigl[\tfrac{1}{6}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k}\mathcal{L}^{2}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top},\,\tfrac{1}{2}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\theta)\bigr)^{\top},\,\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R})\bigr)^{\top}\Bigr]^{\top},&N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq k\leq N_{\beta},\end{cases}

for
\displaystyle(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta
. From Lemma
2
,
3
and (
67
), we have that for
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N_{\beta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{Y}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}4\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\leq N}\int\mu_{k_{1}}^{j_{1}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigl[\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr]_{j_{1}j_{2}}\mu_{k_{2}}^{j_{3}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigl[\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr]_{j_{3}j_{4}}\bigl[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr]_{j_{2}j_{4}}\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=4\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\int\mu_{k_{1}}^{j_{1}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigl[\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr]_{j_{1}j_{2}}\mu_{k_{2}}^{j_{2}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)\equiv 4\int\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx).

We then have, for
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N_{\beta_{S_{1}}}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k_{1}}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{1}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{k_{2}}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{1}})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=720\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k_{1}}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{1}})\bigr)^{\top}a_{S_{1}}^{-1}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{k_{2}}V_{S_{1},0}(x_{S},\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{1}}),

(70)

for
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S_{1}}}+1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N_{\beta_{S}}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{4}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k_{1}}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{k_{2}}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k_{1}}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{2}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{k_{2}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2}\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k_{1}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}S_{1}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{k_{2}}\mathcal{L}V_{S_{1},0}(x,\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k_{1}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{S_{2}S_{2}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{k_{2}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=12\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k_{1}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}})\bigr)^{\top}a_{S_{2}}^{-1}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{k_{2}}V_{S_{2},0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{S_{2}}),

(71)

and for
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N_{\beta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})=\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\bar{H}_{k_{1}k_{2},j}(x,\theta^{\dagger})=\bigl(\partial^{\beta}_{k_{1}}V_{R,0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{R})\bigr)^{\top}\,a_{R}^{-1}(x,\sigma^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\beta}_{k_{2}}V_{R,0}(x,\beta^{\dagger}_{R}),

where
\displaystyle\bar{H}_{k_{1}k_{2},j}\,1\leq j\leq 3
are defined as (
58
), (
59
) and (
60
). Note that we made use of Lemma
4
,
5
to derive (
70
)–(
71
).
For
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1}\leq N_{\beta},\,N_{\beta}+1\leq k_{2}\leq N_{\theta}
, it follows from (
67
), (
68
) and (
69
) that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{Y}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

if
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
. For
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{R}}+1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N_{\theta}
, it follows that if
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},1}(\theta^{\dagger})+\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},2}(\theta^{\dagger})+\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},3}(\theta^{\dagger}),

where we have set:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},1}(\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\leq N}\int[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}\mathscr{W}_{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}j_{4}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},2}(\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\int\Bigl\{[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\log|\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\log|\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})|\Bigr\}\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\leq N}\int\Bigl\{[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Bigr\}\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},3}(\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\equiv\int\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\log|\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})|\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\log|\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})|\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\leq N}\int[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}\,[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}\,[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

with
\displaystyle\mathscr{W}_{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}j_{4}}:\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta\to\mathbb{R}
defined as follows, for

\displaystyle(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\times\Theta
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{W}_{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}j_{4}}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=[\Sigma(x,\theta)]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\Sigma(x,\theta)]_{j_{3}j_{4}}+[\Sigma(x,\theta)]_{j_{1}j_{3}}[\Sigma(x,\theta)]_{j_{2}j_{4}}+[\Sigma(x,\theta)]_{j_{1}j_{4}}[\Sigma(x,\theta)]_{j_{2}j_{3}}.

Notice that we have used (
66
) in the computation of
\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},2}(\theta^{\dagger})
and
\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},3}(\theta^{\dagger})
.
Thus, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2},m}(\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\leq N}\int\Bigl\{[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}[\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\bigl([\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{3}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{2}j_{4}}+[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{4}}[\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{2}j_{3}}\bigr)\Bigr\}\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=2\int\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial^{\theta}_{k_{1}}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\partial^{\theta}_{k_{2}}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})\,\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

where in the second equality, we have used the following formula:

\displaystyle\displaystyle[\partial^{\theta}_{k}\Lambda(\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{2}}=-\sum_{1\leq j_{3}j_{4}\leq N}[\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{1}j_{3}}[\partial^{\theta}_{k}\Sigma(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{3}j_{4}}[\Lambda(x,\theta^{\dagger})]_{j_{4}j_{2}},\ \ N_{\beta}+1\leq k\leq N_{\theta}.

Furthermore, for other cases of
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq N_{\theta}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{Y}}_{k_{1}k_{2}}(x,\theta^{\dagger})=0,

where we have used Lemma
4
and that for
\displaystyle N_{\beta_{S}}+1\leq k\leq N_{\beta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Lambda(x,\theta)\,\mu_{k}(x,\theta)=\Lambda(x,\theta)\begin{bmatrix}\Sigma_{S_{1}R}(x,\theta)\\[2.84544pt]
\Sigma_{S_{2}R}(x,\theta)\\[2.84544pt]
\Sigma_{RR}(x,\theta)\end{bmatrix}a_{R}^{-1}(x,\sigma)\partial^{\beta}_{k}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R})=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{0}_{N_{S_{1}}}\\[2.84544pt]
\mathbf{0}_{N_{S_{2}}}\\[5.69046pt]
a_{R}^{-1}(x,\sigma)\partial^{\beta}_{k}V_{R,0}(x,\beta_{R})\end{bmatrix}.

for
\displaystyle x\in\mathbb{R}^{N},\,\theta=(\beta_{S},\beta_{R},\sigma)\in\Theta
. The proof is now complete.

Appendix E
Proof for Case Study in Section
4.2.1

From (
20
), we have
\displaystyle(\hat{\sigma}_{n})^{2}=F_{1,n}+F_{2,n}+F_{3,n}
, where

\displaystyle\displaystyle F_{1,n}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\equiv\tfrac{6}{\Delta_{n}^{3}}\times\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\bigl(\hat{p}_{i+1}-\hat{p}_{i}-s_{i}\Delta_{n}+s_{i}\tfrac{\Delta_{n}^{2}}{2}\bigr)^{2};

\displaystyle\displaystyle F_{2,n}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\equiv-\tfrac{6}{\Delta_{n}^{2}}\times\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\bigl(\hat{p}_{i+1}-\hat{p}_{i}-s_{i}\Delta_{n}+s_{i}\tfrac{\Delta_{n}^{2}}{2}\bigr)\bigl(s_{i+1}-s_{i}-s_{i}\Delta_{n}\bigr);

\displaystyle\displaystyle F_{3,n}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\equiv\tfrac{2}{\Delta_{n}}\times\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\bigl(s_{i+1}-s_{i}-s_{i}\Delta_{n}\bigr)^{2}

with the hidden components
\displaystyle\hat{p}_{i}
estimated by numerical differentiation:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hat{p}_{i}=\frac{q_{i+1}-q_{i}}{\Delta_{n}},\quad 0\leq i\leq n.

Since the rough component
\displaystyle s_{t}
follows the linear SDE, the solution is explicitly given as: for
\displaystyle u\in[t_{i},t_{i+1})

\displaystyle\displaystyle s_{u}=s_{t_{i}}e^{-(u-t_{i})}+\sigma^{\dagger}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}e^{-(u-v)}dB_{v}

under the true parameter
\displaystyle\sigma^{\dagger}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hat{p}_{i}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=p_{i}+\tfrac{s_{i}}{\Delta_{n}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}e^{-(v-t_{i})}dvdu+\tfrac{\sigma^{\dagger}}{\Delta_{n}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}\int_{t_{i}}^{v}e^{-(v-w)}dB_{w}dvdu,

and then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hat{p}_{i+1}-\hat{p}_{i}=p_{i+1}-p_{i}+\tfrac{1}{\Delta_{n}}\bigl(\Delta_{n}-(1-e^{-\Delta_{n}})\bigr)(s_{i+1}-s_{i})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{\sigma^{\dagger}}{\Delta_{n}}\int_{t_{i+1}}^{t_{i+2}}\int_{t_{i+1}}^{u}\int_{t_{i+1}}^{v}e^{-(v-w)}dB_{w}dvdu-\tfrac{\sigma^{\dagger}}{\Delta_{n}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}\int_{t_{i}}^{v}e^{-(v-w)}dB_{w}dvdu,

where we used:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}e^{-(v-t_{i})}dvdu=\Delta_{n}-(1-e^{-\Delta_{n}}).

Since
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\in[0,1)
is assumed to be small, we use the Taylor expansion for the terms
\displaystyle e^{-\Delta_{n}},\,e^{-(v-w)}
and the stochastic Taylor expansion of
\displaystyle(p_{i+1},s_{i+1})
around
\displaystyle(p_{i},s_{i})
under the true parameter
\displaystyle\sigma^{\dagger}
to obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\hat{p}_{i+1}-\hat{p}_{i}=s_{i}\Delta_{n}+\sigma^{\dagger}B_{t_{i+1}-t_{i}}\tfrac{\Delta_{n}}{2}+\sigma^{\dagger}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}dB_{v}du\\[5.69046pt]
&\qquad+\tfrac{\sigma^{\dagger}}{\Delta_{n}}\int_{t_{i+1}}^{t_{i+2}}\int_{t_{i+1}}^{u}\int_{t_{i+1}}^{v}dB_{w}dvdu-\tfrac{\sigma^{\dagger}}{\Delta_{n}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}\int_{t_{i}}^{v}dB_{w}dvdu+\Delta_{n}^{2}\xi_{i},\end{aligned}

(72)

where the random variable
\displaystyle\{\xi_{i}\}_{i}

appearing in the remainder term satisfies
\displaystyle\mathbb{E}|\xi_{i}|^{2}\leq C
for some constant
\displaystyle C>0
.
We express the Gaussian random variables as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{t_{i+1}-t_{i}}=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\times z_{i}^{(1)},\quad\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}dB_{v}du=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}}\times\Bigl(\tfrac{z_{i}^{(1)}}{2}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(2)}}{2\sqrt{3}}\Bigr),\quad

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}\int_{t_{i}}^{v}dB_{w}dvdu=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{5}}\times\Bigl(\tfrac{z_{i}^{(1)}}{6}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}\Bigr),

where
\displaystyle\{z_{i}^{(j)}\}_{0\leq i\leq n+1,\,j=1,2,3}
is an i.i.d. sequence of standard normal random variables so that it holds

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigl[(B_{t_{i+1}-t_{i}})^{2}\bigr]=\Delta_{n},\quad\mathbb{E}\Bigl[B_{t_{i+1}-t_{i}}\times\Bigl(\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}dB_{v}du\Bigr)\Bigr]=\tfrac{\Delta_{n}^{2}}{2};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigl[B_{t_{i+1}-t_{i}}\times\Bigl(\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}\int_{t_{i}}^{v}dB_{w}dvdu\Bigr)\Bigr]=\tfrac{\Delta_{n}^{3}}{6},\quad\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\Bigl(\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}dB_{v}du\Bigr)^{2}\Bigr]=\tfrac{\Delta_{n}^{3}}{3};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\Bigl(\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}dB_{v}du\Bigr)\times\Bigl(\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}\int_{t_{i}}^{v}dB_{w}dvdu\Bigr)\Bigr]=\tfrac{\Delta_{n}^{4}}{8},\quad\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\Bigl(\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\int_{t_{i}}^{u}\int_{t_{i}}^{v}dB_{w}dvdu\Bigr)^{2}\Bigr]=\tfrac{\Delta_{n}^{5}}{20}.

Then, (
72
) is written as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hat{p}_{i+1}-\hat{p}_{i}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=s_{i}\Delta_{n}+\sigma^{\dagger}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}}\tfrac{z_{i}^{(1)}}{2}+\sigma^{\dagger}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}}\Bigl(\tfrac{z_{i}^{(1)}}{2}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(2)}}{2\sqrt{3}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sigma^{\dagger}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}}\Bigl(\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(1)}}{6}+\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}+\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}\Bigr)-\sigma^{\dagger}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}}\Bigl(\tfrac{z_{i}^{(1)}}{6}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}\Bigr)+\Delta_{n}^{2}\xi_{i}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=s_{i}\Delta_{n}+\sigma^{\dagger}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{3}}\Bigl\{\Bigl(\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(1)}}{6}+\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}+\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}\Bigr)+\Bigl(\tfrac{5z_{i}^{(1)}}{6}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}-\tfrac{z_{i}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}\Bigr)\Bigr\}+\Delta_{n}^{2}\xi_{i}.

(73)

From the ergodicity of the process
\displaystyle\{s_{t}\}_{t}
and (
73
), we have that,
as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta_{n}\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle F_{1,n}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{6(\sigma^{\dagger})^{2}}{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\Bigl(\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(1)}}{6}+\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}+\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}+\tfrac{5}{6}z_{i}^{(1)}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}-\tfrac{z_{i}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}\Bigr)^{2}+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n}R_{i}^{(1)}(\Delta_{n})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\tfrac{23}{5}(\sigma^{\dagger})^{2};

(74)

\displaystyle\displaystyle F_{2,n}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\tfrac{6(\sigma^{\dagger})^{2}}{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\Bigl(\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(1)}}{6}+\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}+\tfrac{z_{i+1}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}+\tfrac{5}{6}z_{i}^{(1)}+\tfrac{z_{i}^{(2)}}{4\sqrt{3}}-\tfrac{z_{i}^{(3)}}{12\sqrt{5}}\Bigr)z_{i}^{(1)}+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n}R_{i}^{(2)}(\Delta_{n})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}-5(\sigma^{\dagger})^{2},

(75)

where each
\displaystyle\{R_{i}^{(1)}(\Delta_{n})\}_{i}
and
\displaystyle\{R_{i}^{(2)}(\Delta_{n})\}_{i}
is sequence of random variables such that for
\displaystyle 0\leq i\leq n
,
\displaystyle j=1,2
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\{\mathbb{E}[|R_{i}^{(j)}(\Delta_{n})|^{2}]\}\leq C\Delta_{n}

for some constant
\displaystyle C>0
. Similarly, we have that

\displaystyle\displaystyle F_{3,n}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2(\sigma^{\dagger})^{2}.

(76)

From (
74
), (
75
) and (
76
), we immediately obtain the convergence (
21
).

Appendix F
Kalman Filter for Sub-Class of (
Hypo-II
)

For simplicity, we write
\displaystyle x_{i}=(x_{S_{1},i},x_{S_{2},i},x_{R,i})\in\mathbb{R}^{N}=\mathbb{R}^{N_{S_{1}}}\times\mathbb{R}^{N_{S_{2}}}\times\mathbb{R}^{N_{R}}
for the state of scheme (
22
) at time
\displaystyle t_{i}
. Component
\displaystyle x_{S_{1},i}
is observable and
\displaystyle h_{i}=(x_{S_{2},i},x_{R,i})\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{H}}
,
\displaystyle N_{H}=N_{S_{1}}+N_{R}
, is the hidden component, in agreement with applications.
Thus, scheme (
22
) is now expressed as

\displaystyle\displaystyle x_{i+1}=b(\Delta_{n},x_{S_{1},i},\theta)+A(\Delta_{n},{x}_{S_{1},i},\theta)h_{i}+w(\Delta_{n},\theta).

(77)

We set
\displaystyle\Sigma(\Delta_{n},\theta)=\mathbb{E}\,[\,w(\Delta_{n},\theta)w(\Delta_{n},\theta)^{\top}]
and assume that
\displaystyle h_{0}|x_{S_{1},0}\sim\mathscr{N}({m}_{0},{Q}_{0})
for some

\displaystyle{m}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{H}}
and
\displaystyle{Q}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{H}\times N_{H}}
. Then, the filtering formula and the marginal likelihood are obtained as follows.

•

Filtering Recursion
: We have that

\displaystyle\displaystyle h_{k}|x_{S_{1},0:k}\sim\mathscr{N}({m}_{k},{Q}_{k}),\quad 0\leq k\leq n,

(78)

with the filter mean
\displaystyle{m}_{k}
and covariance
\displaystyle{Q}_{k}
given as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle{m}_{k}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mu_{H,k-1}+\Lambda_{HS_{1},k-1}\,\Lambda^{-1}_{S_{1}S_{1},k-1}\,\bigl(x_{S_{1},k}-\mu_{S_{1},k-1}\bigr);

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{k}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\Lambda_{HH,k-1}-\Lambda_{HS_{1},k-1}\,\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1},k-1}^{-1}\Lambda_{S_{1}H,k-1},

where
\displaystyle\mu_{H,k-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{H}}
,
\displaystyle\mu_{S_{1},k-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{S_{1}}}
,
\displaystyle\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1},k-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{S_{1}}\times N_{S_{1}}}
,
\displaystyle\Lambda_{S_{1}H,k-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{S_{1}}\times N_{H}}
,
\displaystyle\Lambda_{HS_{1},k-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{H}\times N_{S_{1}}}
,
\displaystyle\Lambda_{HH,k-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{H}\times N_{H}}
are found via the following equations:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mu_{k-1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mu_{S_{1},k-1}\\[2.84544pt]
\mu_{H,k-1}\end{bmatrix}=b(\Delta_{n},x_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)+A(\Delta_{n},x_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)m_{k-1};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Lambda_{k-1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1},k-1}&\Lambda_{S_{1}H,k-1}\\
\Lambda_{HS_{1},k-1}&\Lambda_{HH,k-1}\end{bmatrix}=\Sigma(\Delta_{n},\theta)+A(\Delta_{n},x_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)\,Q_{k-1}\,A(\Delta_{n},x_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)^{\top}.

•

Marginal likelihood
: For a given initial distribution
\displaystyle p_{\theta}(x_{S_{1},0})
, we have that

\displaystyle\displaystyle p_{\theta}(x_{S_{1},0:n})=p_{\theta}(x_{S_{1},0})\times\prod_{k=1}^{n}p_{\theta}(x_{S_{1},k}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1}),

(79)

where
\displaystyle p_{\theta}(x_{S_{1},k}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1})
is the density of
\displaystyle x_{S_{1},k}
given
\displaystyle x_{S_{1},0:k-1}
whose conditional distribution is given by:

\displaystyle\displaystyle x_{S_{1},k}\,|\,x_{S_{1},0:k-1}\sim\mathscr{N}(\mu_{S_{1},k-1},\,\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1},k-1}).

F.1
Derivation of Filter (
78
)

We assume that the filter in the previous time step is obtained as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle h_{k-1}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1}\sim\mathscr{N}(m_{k-1},Q_{k-1}).

(80)

It follows that

\displaystyle\displaystyle p_{\theta}(h_{k}|x_{S_{1},0:k})=\frac{p_{\theta}(x_{k}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1})}{p_{\theta}(x_{S_{1},k}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1})},

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle p_{\theta}(x_{k}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\int p_{\theta}(x_{k},h_{k-1}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1})dh_{k-1}=\int p_{\theta}(x_{k}|x_{k-1})p_{\theta}(h_{k-1}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1})dh_{k-1}.

(81)

From the definition of scheme (
77
), we have that

\displaystyle\displaystyle x_{k}|x_{k-1}\sim\mathscr{N}\bigl(b(\Delta_{n},x_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)+A(\Delta_{n},{x}_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)h_{k-1},\Sigma(\Delta_{n},\theta)\bigr).

(82)

From (
80
), (
81
) and (
82
), we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle x_{k}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1}\sim\mathscr{N}\bigl(\mu_{k-1},\Lambda_{k-1}\bigr),

(83)

where

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mu_{k-1}=b(\Delta_{n},x_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)+A(\Delta_{n},{x}_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)m_{k-1},\qquad\Lambda_{k-1}=\Sigma(\Delta_{n},\theta)+A(\Delta_{n},{x}_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)\,Q_{k-1}\,A(\Delta_{n},{x}_{S_{1},k-1},\theta)^{\top}.

Finally, applying the conditional Gaussian distribution formula, we obtain (
78
).

F.2
Derivation of the Marginal Likelihood (
79
)

From the marginal of the Gaussian distribution (
83
) for
\displaystyle x_{k}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1}
, we immediately obtain:

\displaystyle\displaystyle x_{S_{1},k}|x_{S_{1},0:k-1}\sim\mathscr{N}\bigl(\mu_{S_{1},k-1},\,\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1},k-1}\bigr),

where
\displaystyle\mu_{S_{1},k-1}=\mathrm{proj}_{1,N_{S_{1}}}(\mu_{k-1})
, and
\displaystyle\Lambda_{S_{1}S_{1},k-1}=\bigl[\Lambda_{k-1}^{ij}\bigr]_{1\leq i,j\leq N_{S_{1}}}
.
